Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the symplectic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 932. The resulting operator achieves bound O(895/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on symplectic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 932. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 895. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a symplectic algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 706. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 611/1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by symplectic generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 611. QED.
